<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：心血管疾病风险预测（survival analysis）；场景：跨机构医疗数据协作；传感器/数据类型：人口队列数据（非图像）；目标类别：心血管疾病风险。与遥感/卫星/目标检测无关。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体网络结构，仅提及"deep survival models"。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。未使用剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等任何轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。未讨论参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性、星上/边缘部署等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：Lifelines（148,230人，自报告结果）、Rotterdam Study（10,155人，临床链接结果）；评价指标：C-statistic（一致性统计量）。与遥感数据集（如DOTA、HRSC2016、DIOR、NWPU VHR-10等）无关。

Experiments: 联邦学习训练的深度生存模型优于本地训练模型。Rotterdam Study上C-statistic从0.728提升至0.739；Lifelines上从0.783提升至0.787。

Limitations: 与本研究方向完全无关，无法提供遥感/卫星/目标检测/轻量化方面的局限性分析。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于医疗健康领域的联邦学习应用，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无任何技术或场景上的交集。

Abstract: Cardiovascular disease risk prediction models often rely on data from a single institution or centrally pooled datasets. Extending these models across institutions could be limited by privacy regulations and constraints on sharing patient-level data. Federated learning enables collaborative model development without transferring sensitive patient data, but its application in healthcare remains challenging because datasets often differ in size, population characteristics, and outcome definitions. In this study, we present a federated deep learning approach for privacy-preserving cardiovascular disease risk prediction that integrates two population-based cohorts with different characteristics: Lifelines, including 148,230 participants meeting the study inclusion criteria with self-reported outcomes, and the Rotterdam Study, including a smaller cohort of 10,155 participants with digitally linked clinical outcomes. Model performance was primarily evaluated on the Rotterdam Study because of its complete follow-up. Deep survival models trained using federated learning achieved higher predictive performance than models trained locally without federation. For the Rotterdam Study, the C-statistic increased from 0.728 (95% CI: 0.717-0.739) to 0.739 (95% CI: 0.728-0.749). For Lifelines, the C-statistic increased from 0.783 (95% CI: 0.775-0.791) to 0.787 (95% CI: 0.780-0.792). These findings suggest that federated deep learning across heterogeneous cohorts can improve cardiovascular disease risk prediction while preserving the privacy of individual-level patient data.

</details>


### [251] [Steering Neural Network Training through Interpretable Constraints Based on Partial Dependence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08641)
*Yann Claes,Pierre Geurts,Vân Anh Huynh-Thu*

TL;DR: 提出一种基于偏依赖的解释引导学习方法，使神经网络对特定特征的响应与领域知识对齐，提升模型可解释性和数据效率。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构。仅提及"neural networks"，未给出网络结构细节。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。未讨论参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性或任何边缘/星上部署相关内容。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集。仅提及在"a range of regression problems, including dynamical systems forecasting"上进行实验。评价指标未明确列出。

Experiments: 实验表明：使用该方法训练的模型比无约束模型表现更好且数据效率更高；前者的解释与用户提供的知识对齐，后者则不对齐。未提供定量对比结果。

Limitations: 1. 仅针对回归问题验证，未涉及分类或目标检测任务；2. 需要用户提供先验知识（functional domain knowledge），这在遥感在轨场景中可能难以获取；3. 未讨论计算开销和部署可行性；4. 与遥感、卫星、目标检测、轻量化完全无关。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。唯一可能的间接启发是：如果未来在遥感目标检测中引入可解释性约束（如物理先验引导检测头输出），可参考其偏依赖引导思路，但与本课题核心目标（轻量化、在轨部署）无直接关系。

Abstract: Over the last few years, there has been an increased interest in making machine learning models more interpretable. Although a great deal of effort goes into developing techniques for interpreting the interactions learned by a given model, fewer studies focus on assessing the quality of such explanations. Even fewer focus on how to adjust the model to produce explanations faithful to prior knowledge, a process known as explanation-guided learning. Furthermore, most approaches in this area focus on classification problems and usually assume prior knowledge about which input features or regions are most important. In this work, we introduce a new approach to steering neural networks based on partial dependence, such that their average response to certain features aligns with specific functional domain knowledge about the problem. We empirically demonstrate on a range of regression problems, including dynamical systems forecasting, that models whose training has been controlled using our method perform better than unconstrained models and are more data-efficient. Moreover, we highlight that interpretations obtained from the former actually align with the user-provided knowledge, whereas those obtained from the latter do not.

</details>


### [252] [BiSCo-LLM: Lookup-Free Binary Spherical Coding for Extreme Low-Bit Large Language Model Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08643)
*Yuantian Shao,Peisong Wang,Zhilei Liu,Chuangyi Li,Yuanteng Chen,Pengcheng Xie,Yiwu Yao,Zhihui Wei,Jian Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种无码本的二元球面编码框架BiSCo-LLM，用于大语言模型的极端低位权重量化压缩，通过球面二值化编码+残差BSQ+类别级恢复蒸馏实现高效压缩。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦大语言模型(LLM)的权重量化压缩，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或遥感图像理解。虽然量化方法(weight quantization)属于轻量化技术范畴，但应用场景和目标完全不同，且未涉及遥感在轨部署的任何要素。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测与轻量化方向基本无关，不建议深入阅读。除非后续研究涉及将LLM类量化方法迁移到遥感检测模型，否则优先级极低。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：LLM权重的极端低位压缩(extreme low-bit weight compression)；应用场景：大语言模型部署；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: BiSCo-LLM框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 局部权重块映射到单位超球面并二值化为紧凑球面编码(bit-packed sign stream)；(2) 残差BSQ阶段编码重建误差，提供率-失真路径；(3) 类别级恢复蒸馏(category-wise recovery distillation)，在每个Transformer模块类别替换后执行。另有一个8-bit保护通道路径用于敏感通道的辅助稳定。

Lightweight method: 极端低位权重量化(extreme low-bit weight compression)，具体为二元球面编码(binary spherical coding, BSQ)，属于量化(quantization)范畴。无码本(codebook-free)设计，主要载荷为bit-packed sign stream而非显式VQ质心。包含残差编码和蒸馏恢复策略。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署指标。存储预算包括：二元码、神经解码器、保护通道载荷、LoRA适配器和元数据。但这是针对LLM的存储压缩，与遥感卫星在轨部署无关。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明使用的数据集和评价指标。

Experiments: 摘要未说明具体实验结果和对比方法。

Limitations: (1) 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；(2) 方法针对LLM权重压缩设计，未验证在CNN/检测模型上的适用性；(3) 摘要未报告实际推理速度、硬件部署结果；(4) 未讨论在边缘/星上硬件(FPGA/NPU)上的兼容性。

Ideas for my research: (1) 球面二值化编码的思路可能对遥感检测模型的极端量化有启发，但需要验证在CNN/检测头而非Transformer上的效果；(2) 类别级恢复蒸馏(category-wise recovery distillation)策略可借鉴用于量化后检测模型的微调恢复；(3) 保护通道机制(protected-channel path)的思路可用于遥感检测模型中关键特征层的保护性量化；(4) 整体而言借鉴价值有限，因为方法高度定制于LLM的Transformer结构。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly constrained by memory capacity, weight bandwidth, and checkpoint storage during deployment. Existing low-bit compression methods mainly follow two directions. Scalar or group-wise quantization is simple and compatible with efficient low-precision kernels, but its representation capacity becomes limited when the target budget approaches 2 bits per weight. Vector-quantized weight compression provides a richer block-level representation, but usually introduces explicit codebooks, index lookup, and additional storage accounting. This paper presents BiSCo-LLM, a codebook-free binary spherical coding framework for extreme low-bit LLM weight compression. The core pipeline is built on three components. First, local weight chunks are mapped onto a unit hypersphere and binarized into compact spherical codes, so that the main payload is a bit-packed sign stream rather than explicit VQ centroids. Second, a residual BSQ stage encodes the reconstruction error left by the base spherical codec, providing an explicit rate-distortion path without stored codebooks. Third, category-wise recovery distillation is performed after replacing each Transformer module category, reducing the mismatch between local weight reconstruction and assembled model behavior. A small 8-bit protected-channel path is used as an auxiliary stabilization mechanism for sensitive channels and is counted separately from the BSQ payload. The reported storage budget includes binary codes, neural decoders, protected-channel payloads, LoRA adapters, and metadata.

</details>


### [253] [Multi-Modal, Multi-Environment Machine Teaching for Robust Reward Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08647)
*Ali Larian,Qian Lin,Chang Zong Wu,Daniel S. Brown*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种跨多MDP环境、多模态反馈的分层机器教学算法，用于学习对环境动态鲁棒的奖励函数，解决逆强化学习中奖励过拟合单一环境的问题。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于逆强化学习(IRL)中的奖励函数泛化与机器教学策略，属于机器人/自主智能体的奖励学习领域，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：逆强化学习(IRL)中的奖励函数学习与机器教学；场景：自主智能体跨环境部署；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构。提出了一种分层机器教学算法(hierarchical machine teaching algorithm)，包含两个阶段：1) 贪心选择信息量大的环境；2) 在选定环境中策略性地查询低成本反馈。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。论文未讨论参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性或任何边缘/星上部署相关内容。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集。评价指标：regret（遗憾值）和held-out environments上的泛化性能。

Experiments: 实验表明，在相同反馈预算下，所提方法相比均匀教学基线(uniform teaching baselines)实现了更低的遗憾值和更强的泛化能力。

Limitations: 1) 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关，无法直接借鉴；2) 摘要未说明算法在真实机器人系统上的部署验证；3) 未讨论计算开销和可扩展性。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，基本无可借鉴点。该论文属于强化学习/奖励学习方向，与遥感目标检测和模型轻量化无直接关联。

Abstract: As autonomous agents are increasingly deployed across diverse operational contexts, aligning their behavior with human intent demands reward functions that remain robust to such changes rather than overfitting to any single environment. Inverse reinforcement learning (IRL) provides a principled way to infer such objectives from human feedback. However, existing analyses of optimal teaching approaches for IRL focus on single-environment, demonstration-only settings, leaving underexplored how heterogeneous feedback modalities and environment dynamics jointly constrain reward functions that generalize across multiple environments. Because demonstrations in one MDP entangle reward information with that environments specific structure, the resulting rewards frequently fail to generalize when the agent is deployed in a new setting. We first analyze how different feedback modalities constrain rewards, showing that, in the unlimited-data regime, comparisons impose strictly stronger global constraints than other modalities. Beyond this theoretical analysis, we introduce a hierarchical machine teaching algorithm for reward learning that operates across multiple MDPs. The algorithm first greedily selects informative environments that expose complementary reward constraints, then strategically queries low-cost feedback within those environments. Empirically, our method achieves substantially lower regret and stronger generalization to held-out environments than uniform teaching baselines under identical feedback budgets, demonstrating the importance of multi-environment, multi-modal teaching for learning dynamics-robust reward functions.

</details>


### [254] [Secure Decentralized Federated Learning via Gossip and Virtual Voting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08651)
*Amirhossein Taherpour,Xiaodong Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于有向无环图（DAG）共识的去中心化联邦学习框架gspDAG-FL，利用gossip传播历史实现安全模型聚合，无需中心服务器或区块链协调。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦去中心化联邦学习的安全共识机制，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化任何核心要素。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：去中心化联邦学习安全聚合；应用场景：分布式节点协作训练；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体检测或分类模型架构。实验使用MNIST分类和Penn Treebank语言建模任务，非遥感视觉模型。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。未讨论参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或星上/边缘部署可行性。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：MNIST（手写数字分类）、Penn Treebank（语言建模）；评价指标：学习质量（learning quality）、吞吐量（throughput）、无效来源检测率（invalid-origin detection）。

Experiments: 在N=100节点规模下，gspDAG-FL达到接近基于验证集的账本式FL的学习质量，同时减少协调瓶颈、提高吞吐量，在混合拜占庭和懒惰节点参与下保持高无效来源检测率。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；2. 未涉及任何视觉目标检测任务；3. 未讨论模型轻量化或边缘部署；4. 实验仅在MNIST和语言模型上验证，未在遥感图像或高分辨率航拍数据上测试。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。如果研究方向涉及分布式卫星集群的联邦学习场景，其DAG共识机制可能对多星协同训练的安全聚合有参考意义，但论文本身不涉及遥感目标检测或模型轻量化核心问题。

Abstract: Decentralized federated learning (DFL) removes the central server by letting nodes exchange model updates through peer-to-peer gossip, but existing gossip-based methods often lack provenance finality and resilience to Byzantine or lazy participants. Ledger-assisted federated learning (FL) improves auditability, yet blockchains, shards, or settlement committees can reintroduce global coordination costs that conflict with DFL locality. This paper proposes \emph{gspDAG-FL}, a secure DFL framework that derives consensus from the same gossip history used to disseminate models. Nodes exchange model payloads only with neighbors, while full nodes collect event certificates and receiver-endorsed accepted gossip proofs, reconstruct a compact Topology directed acyclic graph (DAG), and run Hashgraph-style virtual voting followed by compact full-node certificates. Finality is over unique model-origin tuples, not identical local parameter states. To improve resilience, gspDAG-FL combines payload validation, accepted-proof validation, and private semantic audit before aggregation. We formalize the adversarial setting, prove safety and conditional liveness of the control plane, and give a convergence guarantee for certified perturbed gossip under time-varying effective mixing. Experiments on MNIST classification and Penn Treebank language modeling, using fair held-out validation/audit data and networks up to \(N=100\), show that gspDAG-FL achieves learning quality close to validation-based ledger FL while reducing coordination bottlenecks, improving throughput, and maintaining high invalid-origin detection under mixed Byzantine and lazy participation.

</details>


### [255] [EdgeRefine: Privacy-Utility Balance for Graphs via Jaccard Sampling under Edge Differential Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08659)
*Wenxiu Ding,Muzhi Liu,Zheng Yan,Mingjun Wang,Yifan Zhao,Qiao Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出EdgeRefine，一种基于局部差分隐私的图神经网络边级隐私保护框架，通过Jaccard相似度估计边存在概率并自适应加噪，在隐私-效用权衡上优于现有方法。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦图神经网络的边级差分隐私保护，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化中的任何一个核心主题。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：图节点分类与图分类的隐私保护；应用场景：隐私敏感图数据（如引文网络）；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 基于GNN（GCN、GAT等），核心模块为EdgeRefine框架，包含Jaccard相似度估计、边概率排序、基于隐私预算ε的边采样机制。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明轻量化方法。论文不涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS或轻量网络设计。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。论文不涉及星上或边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：ACM、Cora、AMAP（引文网络图数据集）；评价指标：节点分类准确率、图分类准确率、相对绝对误差（对抗图重构攻击）。

Experiments: 在ε=2.5下，EdgeRefine在ACM上（GAT）提升节点分类准确率17.8%，在Cora上（GCN）提升19.7%；图分类相比无噪声基线平均准确率下降约5%；图重构攻击下相对绝对误差均值在Cora上为1.962，AMAP上为1.472。

Limitations: 论文与遥感在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关，无法提供任何直接借鉴；方法针对图结构数据隐私保护，与视觉检测任务无交集。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文属于图学习与隐私计算方向，与遥感在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无交叉。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) have shown considerable success in learning from graph-structured data, but their use in privacy-sensitive areas remains difficult because graph structure can leak sensitive link information. To satisfy edge-level differential privacy, a common approach is to inject noise into all elements of the graph's adjacency matrix, thereby obfuscating the existence of any single edge. However, stronger privacy requires more noise, and excessive noise reduces utility, making the privacy-utility balance a major barrier to practical privacy-preserving graph learning.
  To address this issue, we propose EdgeRefine, a local differential privacy framework that improves this trade-off through adaptive edge refinement. EdgeRefine first estimates edge-existence probabilities using Jaccard similarity and ranks edges for noisy edge removal. To ensure the sparsity and reliability of the final graph, it uses the privacy budget $ε$ to determine the ratio of true to false edges, samples them separately based on this probability ranking, and controls the total number of edges with a separate sampling rate $k$. Extensive experiments show that EdgeRefine achieves accuracy comparable to the noise-free baseline and substantially outperforms other privacy-preserving methods across datasets and GNN architectures. Under privacy budget $ε= 2.5$, EdgeRefine improves node classification accuracy over state-of-the-art baselines by 17.8\% on ACM under GAT and 19.7\% on Cora under GCN. In graph classification, it achieves an average accuracy degradation of around 5\% compared to the noise-free baseline. Under graph reconstruction attacks, EdgeRefine maintains relative absolute error levels above 1 across all privacy budgets, averaging 1.962 on Cora and 1.472 on AMAP, indicating strong resilience against privacy leakage.

</details>


### [256] [Resample or Reroute? Budget-Aware Test-Time Model Selection for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08665)
*Teng-Ruei Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种预算感知的测试时模型选择策略（Resample-or-Reroute, RoR），在LLM推理中动态分配预算于重采样和重路由，以在成本和质量之间取得更优Pareto前沿。

Research relevance: 1分。本文研究对象是大型语言模型（LLM）的推理路由与预算分配问题，与遥感、卫星、目标检测、轻量化部署完全无关。既不是遥感场景，也不是目标检测任务，更不涉及模型轻量化（剪枝、量化、蒸馏等）方法。

Reading priority: 1分。与本研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：LLM推理时的模型选择与路由；应用场景：LLM服务部署；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 不适用。本文不涉及模型结构设计，而是关注多个LLM之间的路由策略。

Lightweight method: 不适用。本文不涉及模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等），而是关注推理时的预算分配策略。

Onboard deployability: 不适用。本文讨论的是云端LLM服务部署的成本-质量权衡，与星上/边缘部署无关。未提及参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等指标。

Datasets and metrics: 四个不同难度的基准测试（benchmarks）；使用多draw正确性张量（multi-draw correctness tensors）进行回放实验；评价指标为成本-质量Pareto前沿。

Experiments: 在11个开源模型的池子上进行回放实验，与single-route、one-commit-router、budget-aware best-of-K、cascade、random-allocation等基线对比，RoR策略在成本-质量Pareto前沿上表现更优，在最具异质性的基准上增益最大。消融实验表明增益受验证器质量门控。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的技术方法或实验范式。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴意义。本文研究的是LLM推理路由问题，与遥感在轨目标检测模型轻量化在问题设定、技术路线、评价体系上均无交集。

Abstract: Routing among large language models (LLMs) trades response quality against serving cost, motivated by the reported gap between deployed routers and a per-instance oracle. Recent analysis shows that test-time resampling can recover per-instance selection headroom that no single-commit router captures; however, that guarantee holds only under an idealized oracle equipped with correctness labels and an unconstrained budget, neither of which a deployed system has. To the best of our knowledge, no previous work treats resampling the committed model and rerouting to an alternative model as competing uses of a single per-query cost budget. Therefore, this work formulates budget-aware test-time model selection: given a per-query budget and an imperfect verifier, allocate each unit of budget between resampling and rerouting so that expected correctness is maximized. An online resample-or-reroute (RoR) allocation policy driven by estimated marginal correctness per unit cost is proposed, and its behavior is grounded in the recoverability asymmetry between selection and sampling. Replay experiments on newly regenerated multi-draw correctness tensors from an eleven-model open-weight pool over four benchmarks of differing difficulty show that the proposed RoR policy attains a favorable cost-quality Pareto front relative to single-route, one-commit-router, budget-aware best-of-K, cascade, and random-allocation baselines for the tested pools, with the largest gains on the most heterogeneous benchmark; an ablation further shows the gains are verifier-gated, shrinking as verifier quality degrades, and robustness replays under a provider price vector and a label-free agreement verifier delineate where the conclusions carry over.

</details>


### [257] [A Practical Investigation of Training-free Relaxed Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08690)
*Guoxuan Xia,Luka Ribar,Paul Balanca*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究无训练松弛式推测解码（relaxed speculative decoding）方法，旨在加速大语言模型自回归采样，通过统一框架对比有损与无损推测解码的性能权衡。

Research relevance: 1分。与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于大语言模型（LLM）的推理加速，属于自然语言处理/生成领域，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或轻量化模型部署。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测与轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：LLM自回归采样加速；应用场景：大语言模型推理；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 采用推测解码框架：主模型（LLM）+ 辅助草稿模型（drafter），草稿令牌由辅助模型生成后由LLM并行验证。

Lightweight method: 推测解码（speculative decoding）作为一种推理加速方法，通过轻量辅助模型生成草稿来加速主模型采样。本文研究的是"松弛式"（relaxed）推测解码，即允许一定精度损失以换取更高加速比。但该方法面向LLM，非视觉模型。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。论文讨论的是LLM推理加速，而非星上/边缘部署场景。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集和评价指标。仅提及在"contemporary settings"上进行基准测试。

Experiments: 摘要未说明具体实验结果和对比方法。仅提及对训练-free松弛推测解码技术进行实践调查，统一现有方法，并提炼经验发现。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；2. 松弛式推测解码依赖草稿模型本身是好的语言模型，不适用于轻量专用多令牌预测草稿器，存在适用性局限；3. 摘要未提供量化加速比或精度损失数据。

Ideas for my research: 弱借鉴点：推测解码（speculative decoding）的思想——用轻量模型生成候选结果再由重模型验证——在遥感在轨场景中可类比为"轻量检测器快速生成候选框 + 重检测器精修"，但需注意：1）遥感目标检测是空间域任务而非自回归序列生成，推测解码的令牌级验证机制不直接适用；2）星上资源受限，同时部署轻量+重量两个模型可能反而增加负担。总体借鉴价值有限。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates sampling from an autoregressive LLM by using a faster auxiliary model to draft tokens which are then verified in parallel by the LLM. Standard speculative decoding is lossless: its rejection and resampling steps exactly preserve the LLM's sampling distribution. Recent work argues that relaxing this strict guarantee can yield further speed-ups, controlled capability-speed trade-offs, or even capability gains. We practically investigate training-free relaxed speculative decoding techniques, unify existing approaches within a shared framework, benchmark them on contemporary settings, and distil takeaways and empirical findings for practitioners. Important takeaways include: relaxation can require considerable capability evaluation unlike lossless speculative decoding, and many relaxed approaches rely on a drafter that is a good language model, making them unsuited for lightweight dedicated multi-token-prediction drafters.

</details>


### [258] [MPFlow: Learning Budgeted Max-Flow Optimization on the Lightning Network with Deep Graph Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08703)
*Harrison Rush,Vincent Davis,Simone Antonelli,Vikash Singh,Jesse Shrader,Emanuele Rossi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究比特币闪电网络中的流动性放置问题，使用图强化学习选择边添加以最大化最大流，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关。

Research relevance: 1分。论文研究的是比特币闪电网络（Bitcoin Lightning Network）中的流动性放置问题，属于区块链/金融网络领域。完全不涉及遥感（remote sensing）、卫星（satellite）、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱、空间在轨智能等任何遥感相关主题；也不涉及目标检测（object detection）、小目标检测、旋转框检测等视觉任务；虽然提到了"lightweight agent"，但这里的轻量是指图神经网络策略网络的轻量，与模型压缩（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS）或边缘/星上部署无关。与本研究方向基本无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：比特币闪电网络中的流动性放置（liquidity placement）组合优化。场景：区块链支付网络。传感器类型：不适用。目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 消息传递策略网络（message-passing policy network）+ 近端策略优化（PPO）+ 动作掩码（action masking）。

Lightweight method: 摘要中提到的"lightweight agent"是指策略网络本身参数量小，但并非遥感领域关注的模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等）。不涉及任何模型压缩或加速技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。该工作已在生产环境中部署用于对等节点推荐，执行了4640次通道开放决策，累计分配267.3 BTC，但这与星上/边缘部署无关。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：真实闪电网络快照（real Lightning Network snapshots）。评价指标：s-t最大流（max-flow）。

Experiments: 在多个种子和未见过的图上，该方法在最大流目标上持续优于强启发式基线。已部署到生产环境。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的遥感相关技术点。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴意义。虽然图强化学习+消息传递网络的方法论在理论上可迁移到其他图优化问题，但与遥感目标检测模型轻量化课题无直接关联。

Abstract: We address liquidity placement in the Bitcoin Lightning Network (LN): given a fixed budget, which channels should a node open to maximize its routing capacity? We cast this as a budget-constrained combinatorial optimization problem on graphs, selecting $k$ edge additions that maximize $s$--$t$ max-flow, a theory-grounded measure of routing capacity, and solve it with graph reinforcement learning. Our lightweight agent combines a message-passing policy network with proximal policy optimization (PPO) and action masking, and is trained under a hub-exclusion curriculum: the network's top hubs are removed from training subgraphs, forcing the policy to learn capacity-aware placement rather than hub attachment. In extensive experiments on real Lightning Network snapshots, our method consistently outperforms strong heuristic baselines on the max-flow objective across multiple seeds and unseen graphs. The agent has been deployed in production for peer recommendations, executing 4640 channel-open decisions that cumulatively allocate 267.3 BTC over $16 million across 30 managed nodes.

</details>


### [259] [Super Weights in LLMs and the Failure of Selective Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08733)
*Shreyas Subramanian,Adewale Akinfaderin,Akarsha Sehwag*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文质疑"超级权重"（Super Weights）概念在LLM剪枝中的普适性，发现单独训练超级权重会导致性能崩溃，而结构化低秩更新（LoRA）远优于孤立地针对重要参数进行微调。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文研究对象是LLM（大语言模型）中的剪枝与参数重要性分析，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或轻量化部署场景。虽然涉及剪枝（pruning）这一轻量化技术，但讨论的是LLM中"超级权重"的剪枝失效问题，而非遥感目标检测模型的轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：LLM参数重要性分析与剪枝有效性验证。应用场景：大语言模型（OLMo-1B, OLMo-7B）。传感器类型：不适用。目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: OLMo-1B和OLMo-7B模型，关注down_proj层和attention权重矩阵。使用LoRA作为结构化微调方法。

Lightweight method: 涉及剪枝（pruning）中的"超级权重"移除方法，但论文结论是该方法对LLM不普适且效果差。LoRA作为参数高效微调方法被提及，但并非模型压缩/轻量化方法本身。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明任何与星上/边缘部署相关的信息，未提及参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署指标。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集和评价指标，仅提到在OLMo-1B和OLMo-7B上进行实验，使用随机猜测水平作为性能基准。

Experiments: 实验表明：单独训练Super Weights（100到8192个参数）导致性能降至随机猜测水平；扩展到局部邻域（最多36K参数）无改善；随机选择相同数量的参数训练反而能提升性能；LoRA仅用0.16%参数即可成功微调；10次随机种子消融实验证实约束LoRA在Super Weight坐标位置更新与无约束结果无统计差异。

Limitations: 1. 完全不涉及遥感/卫星/目标检测场景；2. 仅针对LLM模型，结论对CNN或轻量检测模型的可迁移性未知；3. 未提供任何部署相关指标；4. 未讨论模型压缩后的推理效率。

Ideas for my research: 1. 论文的核心启示是"参数重要性≠参数可训练性"，这对遥感检测模型剪枝有警示意义：不能仅依赖权重幅值或重要性评分来剪枝，需考虑结构化剪枝方法；2. LoRA的结构化低秩更新思路可迁移到遥感检测模型的参数高效微调中；3. 剪枝时需关注参数之间的结构化依赖关系，而非孤立地移除"重要"参数。但整体借鉴价值有限。

Abstract: Recent work identified Super Weights, individual parameters whose removal degrades model performance by orders of magnitude. We show that this degradation due to pruning Super Weights does not universally apply to all LLMs. Furthermore, if these parameters are so important, Super Weight-aware training should be effective. We show the opposite. Training Super Weights in isolation (100 to 8,192 parameters) drops accuracy to random-guessing levels on both OLMo-1B and OLMo-7B, and expanding to local neighborhoods of up to 36K parameters provides no improvement. The failure is specific to Super Weight coordinates: training an equal number of randomly chosen positions in the same down_proj layers instead improves over the baseline, so the collapse comes from targeting Super Weights, not from sparsity itself. Vanilla LoRA, updating every position in attention weight matrices through low-rank structure, succeeds with only 0.16% of parameters, and applying the same low-rank update to down_proj succeeds as well. A 10-seed ablation confirms that constraining LoRA updates at positions corresponding to Super Weight coordinates yields statistically indistinguishable results. These findings establish that parameter importance does not imply parameter trainability in isolation, and that effective fine-tuning relies on structured decompositions over entire layers rather than targeting individually important weights.

</details>


### [260] [Dimensionality Reduction Meets Network Science: Sensemaking on UMAP's kNN Graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08746)
*Duen Horng Chau,Donghao Ren,Fred Hohman,Dominik Moritz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用UMAP降维过程中内部构建的kNN图（而非其2D嵌入）进行数据感知分析，通过PageRank、k-core分解和聚类系数等图算法实现代表性点选取、密度聚类等任务。

Research relevance: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关。论文聚焦于通用高维数据可视化和探索性分析（UMAP的kNN图挖掘），不涉及遥感图像、目标检测、模型轻量化或星上部署中的任何核心问题。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读全文。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：高维数据探索性分析（数据感知、代表性点选取、密度聚类）；应用场景：通用数据可视化；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及；数据集：MNIST, Fashion MNIST（均为通用机器学习数据集，非遥感图像）。

Model architecture: 不涉及深度学习模型架构。核心方法基于UMAP内部构建的kNN图，应用PageRank、k-core分解、聚类系数等经典图算法。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型压缩或加速方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等均未提及）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。未涉及参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等任何部署相关指标。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：MNIST（手写数字）、Fashion MNIST（服装图像）；评价指标：定性评估+定量对比（与k-medoids、HDBSCAN等基线方法对比），具体指标摘要未详细说明。

Experiments: 在MNIST和Fashion MNIST上进行了定量和定性评估，展示了PageRank、k-core、聚类系数三种图分析方法与k-medoids、HDBSCAN等专用方法相比具有竞争力或互补性。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；2. 未涉及任何轻量化或部署问题；3. 仅在MNIST/Fashion MNIST小规模数据集上验证，未在遥感大图或复杂场景下测试；4. 未讨论计算效率或实时性。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义极低。唯一可能的间接启发：如果未来在遥感特征空间中做数据探索或代表性样本选取（如主动学习中的样本选择），UMAP的kNN图分析思路或许有参考价值，但这与本课题核心方向（在轨目标检测模型轻量化）关联非常薄弱。

Abstract: While UMAP is widely used for exploring high-dimensional data, typical workflows focus on its lower-dimensional embedding, largely overlooking the rich k-nearest-neighbor (kNN) graph that UMAP constructs internally. This graph encodes the data manifold in its original high-dimensional space, before the distortion that UMAP's 2D projection introduces. We demonstrate the untapped potential of this internal representation, showing how standard graph algorithms applied to this graph enhance data sensemaking: (1) PageRank identifies representative data points, (2) k-core decomposition reveals dense core regions versus sparse periphery, and (3) clustering coefficient detects tight-knit neighborhoods with highly-similar data points. Through quantitative and qualitative evaluation on MNIST and Fashion MNIST, we show that these graph-based analyses are not only practical but also competitive with or complementary to purpose-built methods (e.g., k-medoids for exemplar selection, HDBSCAN for density-based clustering).

</details>


### [261] [SLORR: Simple and Efficient In-Training Low-Rank Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08754)
*David González-Martínez,Shiwei Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SLORR，一种无状态、保持架构的低秩正则化框架，通过Hoyer稀疏度量和核范数的GPU友好近似，在训练过程中提升模型可压缩性，无需SVD或修改网络结构。

Research relevance: 2分——弱相关。论文聚焦于通用视觉模型（ImageNet）和大语言模型的低秩正则化压缩方法，不涉及遥感/卫星场景，也不涉及目标检测任务。虽然低秩分解是模型轻量化的通用技术，但论文未讨论遥感图像特性（如小目标、旋转目标、多光谱等），也未涉及在轨部署约束（实时性、星上硬件适配）。

Reading priority: 3分——中等优先级。方法本身有轻量化借鉴价值，但论文完全不涉及遥感场景和目标检测任务，需要自行验证迁移效果。如果课题聚焦低秩分解类轻量化方法，值得精读其正则化设计；如果课题更关注剪枝/量化/NAS，则优先级较低。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：图像分类（ImageNet-1K）和语言模型预训练；应用场景：通用视觉和NLP；传感器类型：不涉及遥感传感器；目标类别：不涉及遥感目标。

Model architecture: ResNet-50, ResNet-18, ViT-B/16, ViT-L/16（图像分类）；135M和560M规模LLM（语言模型）。SLORR本身不修改模型架构，仅对原始权重矩阵施加正则化。

Lightweight method: 低秩正则化（low-rank regularization），基于Hoyer稀疏度量和核范数。训练完成后可对模型进行低秩分解压缩。方法特点：无状态（stateless）、保持架构（architecture-preserving）、GPU友好的前向/后向近似计算，无需SVD分解，不引入额外可训练参数。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明星上/边缘部署相关信息。仅提及训练开销：ImageNet实验引入<8%训练开销，LLM实验引入<1%平均训练开销。未报告推理时的参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署关键指标。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：ImageNet-1K（图像分类）；未说明LLM预训练数据集。评价指标：压缩后模型性能保持程度（与未正则化模型对比）。

Experiments: 实验设置：ResNet-50/ViT-B/ViT-L在ImageNet-1K上的短周期继续训练；ResNet-18的完整预训练；135M和560M LLM的预训练。结果：SLORR诱导可压缩性，压缩后模型性能显著优于未正则化模型。

Limitations: 1. 仅在分类任务和语言模型上验证，未涉及目标检测或遥感场景；2. 未报告压缩后的实际推理速度、FLOPs、参数量缩减比例；3. 未讨论在星上硬件（FPGA/NPU）上的适配性；4. 低秩分解对遥感小目标检测任务的有效性未知。

Ideas for my research: 1. SLORR的低秩正则化思想可迁移到遥感目标检测骨干网络（如ResNet、Swin-Tiny）的训练中，在训练阶段诱导权重可压缩性，训练后再进行低秩分解；2. 其"无状态、保持架构"特性意味着不改变检测头结构，对旋转框检测等复杂头部友好；3. 可尝试将SLORR与遥感检测模型（如YOLO系列、RetinaNet）结合，评估在遥感数据集（DOTA、HRSC2016、DIOR）上的压缩效果；4. 需补充星上部署指标（参数量、FLOPs、TensorRT延迟）的评估。

Abstract: Low-rank factorization is widely used to compress neural networks, but modern models are often not naturally amenable to aggressive factorization without significant accuracy loss. Existing training-time low-rank regularizers can improve compressibility, but they often require SVDs of large weight matrices, modify the model architecture (introducing additional trainable parameters), or rely on stateful cached quantities. To address these limitations, we introduce SLORR, a simple, stateless, and architecture-preserving framework for in-training low-rank regularization, instantiated with two main variants based on the Hoyer sparsity metric and the nuclear norm. SLORR directly regularizes the original weight matrices using GPU-friendly approximations for the forward and backward passes of the regularizers, for which we provide approximation guarantees. We first evaluate SLORR on ImageNet-1K across short-horizon continued training of ResNet-50, ViT-B/16, and ViT-L/16, and pretraining of ResNet-18, where SLORR induces compressibility while introducing less than 8% training overhead. We further evaluate SLORR-Hoyer in LLM pretraining at 135M and 560M scales: SLORR-trained compressed models preserve performance substantially better than unregularized models while adding less than 1% average training overhead.

</details>

### [262] [Entropy-Adaptive Multi-Map Chaotic Modulation for Physical-Layer Security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07828)
*Dhrumil Bhatt,Aarish Patel*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出了一种多映射动态熵混沌调制框架（MU-DE-IAEACM-MM），用于多用户无线系统的自适应物理层安全，通过熵调控和异构混沌映射提升抗窃听能力。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦无线通信物理层安全（physical-layer security），涉及混沌调制、熵调控、多用户干扰等通信领域核心问题，完全不涉及遥感（remote sensing）、卫星在轨处理（onboard processing）、目标检测（object detection）或模型轻量化（model lightweighting）。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：物理层安全通信（adaptive physical-layer security）；场景：多用户无线系统、IoT网络；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: MU-DE-IAEACM-MM框架，包含异构混沌映射（Logistic, Tent, Chebyshev, Sine）分布到多用户，熵驱动自适应更新律，基于相关性的入侵检测指标。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何模型轻量化方法（无剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。仅提及在AWGN、衰落、脉冲、有色和窄带干扰下的蒙特卡洛仿真。

Datasets and metrics: 无遥感/视觉数据集。评价指标：BER（误码率）、secrecy capacity（保密容量）、positive secrecy rates。仿真环境：AWGN, fading, impulsive, colored, narrowband interference。

Experiments: 蒙特卡洛仿真，对比固定参数混沌调制方案。支持最多64个合法用户和30个被动窃听者场景，展示了合法与失配接收机之间的持续BER差距及保密容量增益。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关，无法提供任何可借鉴信息。摘要未说明实际硬件实现、计算复杂度、实时性等。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于无线通信物理层安全领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无任何交叉。

Abstract: This paper presents a Multi-Map Dynamic-Entropy Intrusion-Aware Chaotic Modulation (MU-DE-IAEACM-MM) framework for adaptive physical-layer security in multi-user wireless systems. Unlike conventional chaos-based schemes that rely on static parameter secrecy, the proposed architecture treats entropy as a regulated security variable and dynamically updates chaotic control parameters using an entropy-driven adaptation law. Heterogeneous chaotic maps, including Logistic, Tent, Chebyshev, and Sine generators, are distributed across users to enlarge entropy dimensionality and reduce cross-user statistical dependence. A correlation-based intrusion metric is incorporated to detect improved adversarial reconstruction coherence and trigger controlled entropy escalation. Stability analysis establishes bounded convergence conditions for the adaptive update process. Monte Carlo simulations under AWGN, fading, impulsive, colored, and narrowband interference demonstrate a persistent BER gap between legitimate and mismatched receivers and measurable secrecy capacity gains over representative fixed-parameter chaotic modulation schemes. The framework maintains positive secrecy rates in dense deployments with up to 64 legitimate users and 30 passive eavesdroppers. The results indicate that entropy-regulated multi-map chaotic modulation provides a scalable and synchronisation-stable approach for adaptive physical-layer security in next-generation wireless and IoT networks.

</details>


### [263] [Optimal Multi-RIS Placement: Coverage-Guaranteed Sum Rate Maximization Under Inhomogeneous User Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07990)
*Abhishek Rajasekaran,Mehdi Karbalayghareh,Xiaoyan Ma,David J. Love,Christopher G. Brinton*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种分层优化框架，通过贝叶斯优化和超图划分方法，在保证概率覆盖的前提下最小化可重构智能表面(RIS)部署数量，并最大化系统期望和速率。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文研究的是无线通信中RIS（可重构智能表面）的部署位置优化问题，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。虽然"部署"一词在字面上与星上部署有交集，但此处的部署是物理空间中的RIS基站布局，而非深度学习模型在边缘设备上的部署。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：RIS空间部署优化；应用场景：无线通信系统；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。摘要未说明任何遥感或目标检测相关内容。

Model architecture: 不涉及深度学习模型。方法包括：约束集合覆盖问题（constrained set-cover problem）、可见性图（visibility graph）、交超图（intersection hypergraph）上的贪心最小划分、贝叶斯优化（Bayesian Optimization）。

Lightweight method: 不涉及任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等均未提及）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。该论文讨论的是RIS物理部署，而非模型在星上/边缘的部署。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集。评价指标：期望和速率（expected sum rate）、概率覆盖（probabilistic coverage）、部署RIS数量。

Experiments: 提供了数值结果（Numerical results），表明所提框架能一致地识别出同时实现良好覆盖和吞吐量的RIS放置位置，且无不可实际的系统假设。但摘要未给出具体对比方法和消融实验细节。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供任何直接参考。论文聚焦无线通信物理层部署优化，属于通信工程领域。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文与"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题在问题定义、技术方法、应用场景上均无交集。不建议投入时间阅读。

Abstract: The realization of the full potential of Reconfigurable Intelligent Surfaces (RIS) in a wireless system is tied to their strategic spatial deployment. While existing literature primarily focuses on enabling fairness by maximizing coverage to navigate through obstacles, these approaches often fail to exploit the spatial distribution of user density to maximize throughput. Thus, to enable fairness without loss in throughput, we formulate a novel hierarchical problem that maximizes the expected sum rate of the system while guaranteeing probabilistic coverage with the least possible number of RISs deployed. To solve this multi-layered non-convex problem, firstly, we obtain optimal regions where we can deploy RISs to provide the coverage guarantee by solving a constrained set-cover problem on a visibility graph. Then, the minimum number of RISs we require to satisfy the coverage guarantee is obtained by a greedy minimum partitioning on an intersection hypergraph formed using the optimal regions. Finally, a Bayesian Optimization based approach is used to compute the final optimal RIS placement. Numerical results are provided to show that the proposed framework consistently identifies placements that jointly achieve good coverage and throughput, without impractical system assumptions.

</details>


### [264] [SpO$_2$ Predictor-Guided Stage-Wise Time-Frequency Reconstruction of Low-Quality Dual-Wavelength PPG for Oxygen Saturation Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07996)
*Zequan Liang,Elahe Hosseini,Ning Miao,Mahdi Pirayesh Shirazi Nejad,Wei Shao,Ehsan Kourkchi,Setareh Rafatirad,Houman Homayoun*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种SpO₂预测器引导的时频域两阶段PPG信号重建框架，用于低质量可穿戴PPG信号的血氧饱和度估计。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于可穿戴PPG信号的血氧饱和度(SpO₂)估计与信号重建，属于生物医学信号处理领域，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感核心内容，也不涉及目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：SpO₂估计与PPG信号重建；场景：可穿戴健康监测；传感器：双波长PPG传感器；目标类别：无目标检测相关内容。

Model architecture: 两阶段框架：1) SpO₂预测器（预训练）；2) 掩码重建模型（时域波形损失+STFT频域损失+SpO₂预测器引导约束）。共四个训练阶段。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法（无剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等内容）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。该工作面向可穿戴设备，但未讨论模型轻量化或边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：OpenOximetry Repository（公开）+ 私有可穿戴PPG数据集；指标：subject-level MAE（平均绝对误差），公开集2.882%，私有集2.359%。

Experiments: 实验表明所提方法在公开和私有数据集上均取得最低subject-level MAE。摘要未提供与基线方法的详细对比或消融实验。

Limitations: 1) 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；2) 未讨论模型复杂度或推理效率；3) 未说明模型参数量、计算量等轻量化关键指标；4) 仅针对PPG信号重建，领域差异极大。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴意义有限。但以下思路可抽象迁移：1) 预测器引导的联合优化思想——类似地，在遥感目标检测中可用检测器作为引导，约束轻量化模型的特征重建或剪枝过程，保留检测相关语义信息；2) 时频域联合损失——遥感图像中可结合空域与频域损失（如FFT/DCT域）来增强轻量化模型对高频细节（小目标）的保持能力。但整体而言，该论文对"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题的直接参考价值很低。

Abstract: Continuous oxygen saturation (SpO$_2$) estimation from wearable photoplethysmography (PPG) is important for long-term health monitoring, but low-quality red and infrared PPG segments can distort waveform morphology and degrade SpO$_2$ prediction accuracy. Existing PPG denoising and reconstruction methods usually optimize waveform fidelity or heart rate characteristics, while time-domain waveform loss on PPG signals alone insufficiently preserves frequency structure and SpO$_2$-relevant information. This paper proposes a SpO$_2$ predictor-guided stage-wise time-frequency reconstruction framework for low-quality dual-wavelength PPG signals. The proposed method first selects high-quality PPG segments to pretrain a SpO$_2$ predictor. A masked reconstruction model is then trained to recover randomly masked PPG regions using a joint reconstruction objective that combines time-domain waveform loss with frequency-domain loss computed from the short-time Fourier transform (STFT). To make the reconstruction task physiologically relevant, the pretrained SpO$_2$ predictor is incorporated as an additional constraint, encouraging the reconstructed PPG to preserve SpO$_2$ information rather than only minimizing waveform reconstruction error. The SpO$_2$ predictor and PPG reconstructor model are optimized through four training stages. Experiments on the public OpenOximetry Repository and a private wearable PPG dataset show that the proposed approach achieves the lowest subject-level MAE, with 2.882\% on the public dataset and 2.359\% on the private dataset.

</details>


### [265] [Low-Rate Wrist SpO2 Estimation under Micro-Perturbations Using Motion-Aware Beat Selection and Perfusion-Guided Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08001)
*Zequan Liang,Ning Miao,Wei Shao,Mahdi Pirayesh Shirazi Nejad,Ehsan Kourkchi,Hossein Sayadi,Setareh Rafatirad,Houman Homayoun*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种轻量级低采样率腕部血氧饱和度(SPO2)估计框架，通过运动感知心跳选择和灌注引导校准，在低采样率下实现高精度估计并降低功耗。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于可穿戴健康传感中的血氧饱和度(SPO2)估计，使用PPG信号和加速度计数据，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感核心要素；不涉及目标检测任务；虽然提到了"lightweight"和"energy-efficient"，但轻量化对象是PPG信号处理框架而非视觉模型，且不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等模型压缩方法，也不涉及FPGA/NPU/TensorRT等边缘部署技术。

Reading priority: 1分，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：生理信号回归估计（SPO2估计）；应用场景：可穿戴健康监测（腕部）；传感器类型：PPG（光电容积描记）双波长传感器 + 加速度计；目标类别：无（非视觉目标检测任务）。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构。框架包含：运动感知心跳选择模块（利用加速度计计算运动评分对心跳加权）、灌注引导校准模块（利用受试者特异性灌注参考进行校准）、以及基于双波长PPG信号的beat-level AC/DC分量提取和R值计算。

Lightweight method: 轻量化体现在低采样率设计（25 Hz vs 100 Hz），通过降低PPG传感器采样率来减少功耗，实现energy-efficient wearable implementation。不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等模型压缩方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、显存、延迟等部署指标。仅提及在25 Hz采样率下性能与100 Hz相当，且降低了PPG传感器功耗。该框架面向可穿戴设备而非星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：私有可穿戴数据集（private wearable dataset）；评价指标：MAE（平均绝对误差）、RMSE（均方根误差），具体为MAE 2.305±1.113%，RMSE 3.117±1.743%。

Experiments: 在私有可穿戴数据集上，所提方法在25 Hz采样率下达到最佳性能（MAE 2.305±1.113%，RMSE 3.117±1.743%），且与100 Hz采样率性能相当，验证了低采样率框架的有效性。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关，无法直接借鉴；2. 使用私有数据集，无法复现；3. 未提供模型参数量、FLOPs等计算资源指标；4. 仅针对腕部SPO2估计这一特定生理信号任务，方法通用性有限。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义非常有限。唯一可能参考的思路是：在遥感在轨场景中，是否可以通过降低传感器采样率/分辨率来减少数据量和功耗，同时通过信号处理或校准方法补偿性能损失。但这与模型轻量化（压缩网络本身）是不同的技术路线。

Abstract: Continuous oxygen saturation (SPO2) monitoring from photoplethysmography (PPG) is important for wearable health sensing, but wrist-based SPO2 estimation remains challenging due to subtle wrist micro-perturbations and inter-subject differences in local perfusion status. These factors can destabilize the red-to-infrared ratio-of-ratios (R) and reduce the reliability of conventional fixed R-SPO2 mapping. This paper proposes a lightweight low-rate wrist SPO2 estimation framework that integrates motion-aware beat selection and perfusion-guided calibration. The proposed method extracts beat-level alternating-current/direct-current (AC/DC) components from dual-wavelength PPG signals, computes beat-level R values, and uses accelerometer-derived motion scores to weight beats within each sliding window. A subject-specific perfusion reference is further used to guide calibration across different perfusion conditions. Experiments on a private wearable dataset show that the proposed method achieves the best 25 Hz performance, with an MAE of 2.305$\pm$1.113% and an RMSE of 3.117$\pm$1.743%, while maintaining performance comparable to the 100 Hz sampling rate and reducing PPG sensor power consumption for energy-efficient wearable implementation. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed framework for low-rate wrist SPO2 estimation under micro-perturbations.

</details>


### [266] [Unit-Independent Low-Rate Wrist GSR Processing for Stress Detection Using Phasic nSCR Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08007)
*Zequan Liang,Sally Hang,Geneva M. Jost,Ning Miao,Wei Shao,Mahdi Pirayesh Shirazi Nejad,Hossein Sayadi,Ehsan Kourkchi,Setareh Rafatirad,Camelia E. Hostinar,Houman Homayoun*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种基于腕部皮肤电反应(GSR)的低速率、单位无关的信号处理流程，用于可穿戴压力检测，通过提取每分钟皮肤电反应次数(nSCR/min)作为压力特征，在TSST压力测试任务中达到0.823-0.871的平衡准确率。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文研究的是可穿戴设备上的压力检测（生理信号处理），完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱图像，也不涉及目标检测或模型轻量化。唯一可能的技术交叉点是信号处理流程中的降采样（从100Hz到25Hz），但这与遥感模型轻量化（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）完全不同。

Reading priority: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文属于可穿戴生理信号处理领域，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：压力检测（stress detection）；场景：可穿戴设备（wrist-based GSR）；传感器：腕部GSR传感器（We-Be GSR）；目标类别：压力状态二分类（TSST vs 坐姿/站姿基线）。与遥感目标检测无关。

Model architecture: 随机森林（Random Forest）分类器。信号处理流程包括：原始GSR信号清洗 → 分解为tonic SCL和phasic SCR → 鲁棒z-score归一化 → phasic SCR峰值检测 → 计算nSCR/min。与遥感目标检测模型无关。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等）。仅涉及信号采样率从100Hz降低到25Hz（低速率处理），但这属于信号采集层面的降采样，不是模型压缩或加速方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。仅提及25Hz低采样率处理在可穿戴设备上的可行性，但未给出具体计算资源需求。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：自采集的配对腕部和手掌GSR数据，来自31名参与者，包含坐姿基线、站姿基线、中性说话和TSST四种场景。评价指标：平衡准确率（balanced accuracy）。25Hz We-Be GSR特征在TSST vs 坐姿基线任务上达到0.823，在TSST vs 站姿基线任务上达到0.871。

Experiments: 主要实验：比较25Hz低采样率特征与原始100Hz特征的性能，结果显示25Hz特征在各项任务上取得了与100Hz特征相当的平衡准确率。未涉及遥感目标检测领域的对比实验。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无直接局限性可讨论。从可借鉴角度看，该论文未涉及任何遥感场景、目标检测模型或轻量化技术。

Ideas for my research: 该论文与"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题基本无直接可借鉴点。唯一的间接启发是：在资源受限设备上，可以通过降低输入数据采样率/分辨率来减少计算量（类似该论文从100Hz降到25Hz），但这在遥感领域已是常识性做法（降低输入图像分辨率），且会带来检测精度损失，需要权衡。

Abstract: Galvanic skin response (GSR) is widely used for stress detection, but wrist-based GSR remains challenging because its absolute amplitude can differ substantially from laboratory-grade palmar measurements. In this paper, we propose a unit-independent low-rate wrist GSR processing pipeline to extract the number of skin conductance responses per minute (nSCR/min) as a stress-related feature. We collect paired wrist and palmar GSR recordings from 31 participants during sitting baseline, standing baseline, neutral speaking, and the Trier Social Stress Test (TSST), a laboratory social stressor task. The proposed pipeline cleans the raw GSR signal, decomposes it into tonic skin conductance level (SCL) and phasic skin conductance response (SCR), applies robust z-score normalization, and detects phasic SCR peaks to compute nSCR/min. Using random forest on 25Hz We-Be GSR, nSCR/min achieved balanced accuracies of 0.823 and 0.871 for binary classification between TSST and the sitting and standing baselines, respectively. Moreover, the 25Hz We-Be GSR features achieved comparable balanced accuracy to the original 100Hz features across the evaluated tasks. These results suggest the feasibility of low-rate, unit-independent wrist GSR processing for wearable stress detection.

</details>


### [267] [DKDNet: Dual Knowledge and Data-Driven Network for Cross-Domain Automatic Modulation Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08031)
*Shuang Wang,Chenxu Wang,Hantong Xing,Hanlin Mo,Lirong Han,Licheng Jiao*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种基于信号先验知识的双知识数据驱动网络(DKDNet)，用于跨域自动调制分类(AMC)，通过多表示特征编码器和动态轻量融合单元实现统一表示学习与自适应特征融合。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文研究的是通信领域的自动调制分类(AMC)，涉及信号处理与跨域泛化，完全不涉及遥感图像、卫星对地观测、目标检测或模型轻量化部署等核心主题。虽然文中提到了"轻量融合单元"(lightweight fusion unit)，但这是针对信号特征融合的轻量设计，与遥感目标检测模型的轻量化（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）无关。

Reading priority: 1分。该论文研究通信调制分类，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：自动调制分类(AMC)；应用场景：通信环境下的跨域信号分类；传感器类型：通信信号接收设备（非遥感传感器）；目标类别：调制方式类别（非遥感目标）。摘要未说明具体调制类别数量。

Model architecture: DKDNet (Dual Knowledge and Data-driven Network)，包含两个核心模块：MRFE (Multi-Representation Feature Encoder，多表示特征编码器) 和 DLFU (Dynamic Lightweight Fusion Unit，动态轻量融合单元)。输入为三种先验引导的信号表示：IQ (同相/正交)、AP (幅度-相位)、ACF (自相关函数)。

Lightweight method: 文中仅提及"动态轻量融合单元(DLFU)"这一轻量设计，但未说明具体的轻量化技术（如剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。摘要未说明模型参数量、FLOPs、推理速度等轻量化指标。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。虽然DLFU被描述为"轻量"，但缺乏量化数据支撑其在星上/边缘部署的可行性。

Datasets and metrics: 使用了模拟数据集和公开数据集进行验证。摘要未说明具体数据集名称、评价指标（如分类准确率等）。

Experiments: 在模拟和公开数据集上验证了先验选择的合理性，并证明了所提方法的优越性。摘要未提供具体数值结果或对比方法名称。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；2. 轻量化仅体现在模块名称中，缺乏具体指标；3. 未讨论在轨部署、实时推理等关键问题；4. 实验细节不足，无法评估方法有效性。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义有限。可考虑的点：1) 多表示特征融合的思路（MRFE+DLFU）或许可迁移到遥感多模态数据（光学+SAR+高光谱）的特征融合中；2) 利用领域知识（先验）指导特征学习的思路，在遥感领域可类比为利用地物物理特性/几何先验辅助检测。但整体相关性极低。

Abstract: The dynamics of communication environments induce significant distribution shifts across domains, challenging the generalization of deep learning-based automatic modulation classification (AMC) models. While existing UDA methods alleviate this problem by aligning source and target features, they give limited consideration to modulation-specific structures that remain informative across domain conditions. In this paper, we consider signal prior knowledge, grounded in communication protocols and physical principles, as a potential way to enhance cross-domain representation learning. Given that different priors may vary in modulation discriminability, domain stability, and complementarity, this paper first analyzes five commonly adopted signal representations that instantiate different signal priors. From them, in-phase/quadrature (IQ), amplitude--phase (AP), and autocorrelation function (ACF) are selected as compact prior-guided inputs. Based on that, a dual knowledge and data-driven network (DKDNet) is proposed for cross-domain AMC. The multi-representation feature encoder (MRFE) and dynamic lightweight fusion unit (DLFU) are designed to achieve unified representation learning and adaptive feature fusion, and the resulting fused features are optimized with modulation classification and adversarial domain alignment objectives. Experiments on both simulated and public datasets validate the rationality of the prior selection and demonstrate the superiority of the proposed method.

</details>


### [268] [Beyond Single-Band: Analysis and Resource Allocation for Multi-band ISAC Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08068)
*Haotian Liu,Zhiqing Wei,Xingwang Li,Yunxin Geng,Qixun Zhang,Zhiyong Feng*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出多频段ISAC系统的检测概率与虚警概率闭式解，并设计基于ADMM的联合功率与时频资源分配方案以最大化检测性能。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于6G无线通信中的多频段集成感知与通信（ISAC）系统，不涉及遥感卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感领域，也不涉及目标检测模型、轻量化或星上部署。

Experiments: 通过数值结果验证了闭式推导的准确性，多频段信号相比单频段基线在90%检测概率下实现了18 dB检测增益。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向完全无关。论文属于通信信号处理领域，不涉及视觉目标检测、遥感图像处理或模型压缩部署。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文属于通信感知一体化（ISAC）领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化的研究方向无交集。

Abstract: Integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) has emerged as a pivotal technology for sixth-generation wireless networks to empower high-precision sensing. The demand for superior sensing resolution and the reality of spectrum fragmentation have driven the research of multi-band ISAC. Multi-band ISAC provides frequency diversity through independent observations across disparate bands, mitigating sensing performance fluctuations caused by frequency-selective radar cross-section compared to single-band counterparts. In this paper, we propose a framework for analytical performance characterization and resource optimization in multi-band ISAC systems. Specifically, analytical closed-form detection and false alarm probabilities for multi-band OFDM signals are derived, providing a theoretical foundation for subsequent resource allocation. Then, a joint power and time-frequency resource allocation scheme is developed and solved via a proposed ADMM-based algorithm to maximize detection performance. Numerical results validate the accuracy of the closed-form derivations and demonstrate the superior robustness of multi-band signals. Notably, the proposed optimization scheme achieves an 18 dB detection gain over traditional single-band baselines at a 90\% detection probability.

</details>


### [269] [Low-Complexity Gridless Single-Snapshot DoA Estimation via Truncated Hankel Newton-MUSIC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08108)
*Ruoxiao Cao,Wentao Yu,Yi Gong,Khaled B. Letaief*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种低复杂度的截断Hankel Newton-MUSIC框架，用于可重构天线ISAC系统中的单快拍DoA估计，通过固定行维度的截断Hankel矩阵降低计算复杂度，并利用牛顿更新消除网格量化误差。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于通信感知一体化(ISAC)系统中的波达方向(DoA)估计，属于信号处理领域，不涉及遥感图像、卫星观测、目标检测或模型轻量化。虽然涉及"低复杂度"和"实时性"，但方法本质是阵列信号处理中的子空间算法加速，与遥感在轨目标检测的轻量化（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、轻量网络等）无直接关联。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不推荐阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Model architecture: 截断Hankel Newton-MUSIC框架：构造固定行维度的截断Hankel矩阵恢复有效信号子空间，结合牛顿二阶更新在连续角度域精化粗网格估计。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等典型轻量化方法。仅涉及算法层面的低复杂度设计：通过截断Hankel矩阵固定行维度，使复杂度随天线端口数线性增长（而非平方或立方），属于信号处理算法加速，非深度学习模型轻量化。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、显存、能耗等部署指标。仅提及对于大阵列，相比传统方阵Hankel MUSIC，运行时间降低两个数量级以上，适合ISAC系统实时感知。但未涉及星上/边缘部署可行性。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集。评价指标：DoA估计精度（接近方阵Hankel Newton-MUSIC）、运行时间（runtime reduction）。

Experiments: 仿真实验表明：所提方法在DoA估计精度上接近方阵Hankel Newton-MUSIC，同时大幅降低运行时间；大阵列场景下相比传统方阵Hankel MUSIC实现两个数量级以上加速。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关，不涉及任何遥感图像或视觉检测任务；2. 方法基于信号子空间分解，不适用于深度学习目标检测模型的轻量化；3. 未提供参数量、FLOPs等深度学习部署关键指标；4. 仅在仿真数据上验证，未涉及真实遥感场景。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义有限。唯一可参考的思路是：在信号/矩阵处理层面通过"截断"操作降低复杂度的思想，或许可启发遥感检测模型中特征图或注意力矩阵的近似低秩分解加速。但整体上该论文与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向关联极弱，不建议深入研读。

Abstract: Reconfigurable antenna arrays can provide enhanced spatial Degrees of Freedom (DoFs) for Integrated Sensing And Communication (ISAC) systems, enabling high-resolution Direction of Arrival (DoA) estimation. In highly dynamic scenarios, however, DoA estimation must be performed within short coherence intervals, which often restricts processing to a single snapshot. Conventional subspace methods then suffer from rank deficiency, while Hankel-based spatial smoothing incurs high computational cost when the array size is large. This paper proposes a low-complexity gridless Truncated Hankel NewtonMUSIC framework for single-snapshot DoA estimation. The proposed method constructs a truncated Hankel matrix with a fixed row dimension to recover an effective signal subspace while reducing the cost of correlation construction and subspace decomposition. When the truncation length is independent of the array size, the dominant complexity scales linearly with the number of antenna ports. To reduce grid-induced quantization errors, coarse grid estimates are further refined by a secondorder Newton update in the continuous angular domain. Simulation results show that the proposed method achieves DoA estimation accuracy close to square Hankel Newton-MUSIC while substantially reducing runtime. For large arrays, it provides more than two orders of magnitude runtime reduction compared with conventional square Hankel MUSIC, making it suitable for realtime sensing in reconfigurable antenna-enabled ISAC systems.

</details>


### [270] [Unified Analytical Model for Atomic Receivers Under Typical Quantum Interference Paths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08118)
*Yiyue Xiang,Neng Ye,Qihao Peng,Pei Xiao,Jianping An*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出原子接收器的统一分析模型，利用量子干涉（EIT）进行射频-光信号转换，推导三种量子干涉路径的等效信道增益闭式表达式。

Research relevance: 1分。与本研究方向基本无关。论文研究的是基于量子干涉（EIT）的原子接收器在无线通信中的信道建模，属于量子通信/量子传感领域，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明。论文提出的是信道分析模型（QCTC），非深度学习或神经网络模型。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于量子通信物理层理论分析，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无任何交集。

Abstract: Atomic receivers, which leverage the quantum interference termed electromagnetically induced transparency (EIT) for radio-frequency (RF) to optical signal transduction, offer a revolutionary paradigm for next-generation wireless communications. However, current information-theoretic characterizations are predominantly restricted to the Ξ-type of EIT path and rely heavily on the weak-probe approximation, which fails to predict the behavior of the atomic receivers under high signal-to-noise ratio regimes. In this paper, we establish a unified analytical model for atomic receivers, and apply this model to three typical quantum interference paths, i.e., V -type, Λ-type, and Ξ-type configurations. To provide a universal characterization, we propose the quantum coherence transfer coefficient (QCTC) to model the equivalent channel response induced by atomic receivers, using a steady-state perturbation framework built on the three-level EIT solution. The closed-form expressions of equivalent channel gains are then derived for three paths. Our results provide an analytical foundation for future capacity analysis and waveform optimization in atomic radio communication.

</details>


### [271] [GenAI-Enhanced Digital Twins for Predictive Interference Management in Ultra-Dense Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08141)
*Afan Ali,Ali Arshad Nasir,Daniel Benevides da Costa*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种生成式AI增强的数字孪生框架，用于超密集室内下一代网络的主动式稀有事件信道合成与鲁棒预编码。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于无线通信领域的超密集室内网络信道建模与预编码，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测或模型轻量化。虽然使用了生成对抗网络（cGAN），但应用场景和问题域与遥感卫星在轨目标检测轻量化无任何交集。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

Model architecture: cGAN框架，包含spatio-temporal generator（时空生成器）和PatchGAN discriminator（PatchGAN判别器）。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法（如剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明星上/边缘部署相关信息。仅提到推理开销为2.8-4.1 ms，控制信道开销约2.1 kB/10 ms slot，但这是针对通信基站场景，与遥感卫星在轨部署无关。

Datasets and metrics: Sionna仿真平台；评价指标：SINR（信干噪比）、packet-loss（丢包率）、CSI oracle gap（信道状态信息理想差距）。

Experiments: Sionna仿真验证：中值SINR提升5-8 dB，丢包率降低60-70%，完美CSI理想差距缩小60-85%。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可参考的遥感、目标检测或轻量化内容。仅cGAN的生成式架构在方法论层面有极弱的间接参考价值。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。如果强行寻找间接联系：cGAN中的PatchGAN判别器设计思路或许可用于遥感图像生成/增强任务，但与本课题（在轨目标检测模型轻量化）的核心问题（模型压缩、加速、边缘部署）无关。

Abstract: Ultra-dense indoor next-generation networks suffer severe interference from mobility-induced blockages and localized multi-user hotspots that conventional digital twins~(DTs) cannot anticipate. We propose a generative AI~(GenAI)-enhanced DT framework employing a conditional generative adversarial network~(cGAN) with a spatio-temporal generator and PatchGAN discriminator for proactive rare-event channel synthesis. A worst-case zero-forcing~(WC-ZF) beamformer driven by Monte Carlo synthetic trajectories realizes distributionally robust precoding, with control-channel overhead bounded to $\approx$2.1\,kB per 10\,ms slot. Sionna-based simulations confirm a 5--8\,dB median signal-to-interference-plus-noise-ratio (SINR) gain, 60--70\% packet-loss reduction, and 60--85\% closure of the perfect channel state information (CSI) oracle gap within a 2.8--4.1\,ms inference overhead.

</details>


### [272] [Doppler-Resilient Rydberg Atomic Receiver for High-Dynamic Communication Networks via Adaptive Local Oscillator Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08145)
*Yiyue Xiang,Jianxiong Pan,Qiaolin Ouyang,Bichen Kang,Bin Qi,Neng Ye*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种自适应本振跟踪的里德堡原子接收机架构，利用CPAFC算法动态估计多普勒频移并反馈调整本振频率，使信号始终锁定在原子响应带宽内，以缓解高动态环境（如卫星通信）下的性能恶化。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于Rydberg原子接收机的通信架构设计，属于无线通信物理层/射频前端领域，不涉及遥感图像、目标检测、模型轻量化或星上部署等核心内容。虽然提到了"satellite communications"（卫星通信），但仅作为高动态场景的应用背景，并非遥感卫星在轨智能处理方向。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读全文。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：自适应频率跟踪与接收机架构设计；应用场景：高动态卫星通信；传感器类型：Rydberg原子接收机（非光学/雷达成像传感器）；目标类别：不涉及目标检测。摘要未说明遥感、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱等任何遥感相关内容。

Model architecture: 提出自适应本振跟踪Rydberg原子接收机架构，核心模块包括：交叉积自动频率控制（CPAFC）算法、频率误差估计器、本振反馈控制环路。不涉及任何深度学习模型或神经网络结构。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集。评价指标：未明确列出，但从描述看应包含误码率、频率锁定精度等通信性能指标。采用仿真实验验证。

Experiments: 通过仿真验证，所提架构在高动态环境下显著优于现有Rydberg原子接收机，能有效缓解性能恶化。摘要未提供具体数值对比结果。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向完全无关；2. 仅提供仿真结果，缺乏硬件实测验证；3. 未讨论计算复杂度、功耗等实际部署关键指标；4. 未说明CPAFC算法的收敛速度与跟踪精度边界。

Abstract: Rydberg atomic receiver has emerged as promising candidate for next-generation wireless communication, due to the exceptional sensitivity and ability to overcome the physical limitations of traditional radio frequency antennas. Utilizing the resonant response of atomic energy levels for signal detection, Rydberg atomic receiver is inherently confined to a narrow instantaneous bandwidth. However, in high-mobility scenarios such as satellite communications, the severe Doppler effect induces carrier frequency offsets, which drive the signal beyond the instantaneous bandwidth and result in severe distortion. In this paper, we propose an adaptive local oscillator (LO) tracking Rydberg atomic receiver architecture designed to lock high-dynamic signals within the effective atomic response bandwidth. By employing a cross-product automatic frequency control (CPAFC) algorithm, the system dynamically estimates the instantaneous frequency offset, generates a corresponding error control signal, and adjusts the LO frequency through a feedback loop. Consequently, the intermediate frequency signal can always be locked close to the center of the atomic response bandwidth regardless of dynamics. Simulation results show that the proposed architecture significantly outperforms existing Rydberg atomic receiver, effectively alleviating performance degradation in high-dynamic environments.

</details>


### [273] [Fundamental Sensing Limits of 6G Cooperative MIMO-ISAC Networks: Joint Position-Velocity CRLB and Decoupling Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08510)
*Yanpeng Su,Norman Franchi,Maximilian Lübke*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Cramér-Rao下界（CRLB）的合作MIMO通感一体化（ISAC）网络性能界分析方法，推导了位置与速度的完整Fisher信息矩阵及简化CRLB。

Research relevance: 1分。与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于合作MIMO ISAC网络的CRLB理论分析，属于通信与感知融合的底层信号处理理论，不涉及遥感图像、目标检测模型、轻量化或星上部署等核心内容。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：CRLB性能界分析；场景：合作MIMO ISAC网络；传感器类型：MIMO雷达/通信一体化系统；目标类别：位置与速度估计（非视觉目标检测）。摘要未说明遥感、卫星、光学/SAR/高光谱图像等遥感场景。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明。本文不涉及深度学习模型或神经网络架构。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。本文不涉及模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。文中提到的"简化CRLB"是指对Fisher信息矩阵的数学简化以降低计算复杂度，而非神经网络压缩。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。不涉及参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署指标。文中仅提及简化CRLB具有低计算复杂度，但这是理论分析层面的复杂度，非深度学习模型部署指标。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：仿真数据（非遥感图像数据集如DOTA、HRSC2016、DIOR等）；评价指标：位置CRLB、速度CRLB、简化误差。

Experiments: 通过仿真验证了简化CRLB在一般情况下的适用性，分析了资源和几何参数对位置/速度误差界的影响。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向完全无关；2. 不涉及任何视觉目标检测任务；3. 不涉及任何深度学习模型；4. 不涉及星上部署或边缘推理。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值极低。唯一可能的间接联系是：ISAC（通感一体化）是未来卫星通信与遥感融合的趋势，但本文纯理论分析，不涉及具体遥感检测模型或轻量化方法，不建议作为主要参考。

Abstract: This paper presents a Cramér-Rao lower bound (CRLB)-based performance bound analysis of cooperative multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) integrated sensing and communications (ISAC) networks. We first show the CRLB transformation of the signal-level parameters to the state parameters (position and velocity) in cooperative ISAC networks. Unlike existing studies that primarily ignored coupling between position and velocity in the Fisher information matrix (FIM), we derive the full FIM and the corresponding exact CRLB. Particularly, the results of multi-monostatic sensing, multi-bistatic sensing, and their hybrid are discussed. Addressing the complexity and tractability, we simplify the FIM and CRLB by excluding the coupling terms between the position and velocity, and provide a criterion for determining whether the simplification is valid. The simplified CRLB benefits from low computational complexity and provides a tractable and reliable performance metric for optimization problems such as resource allocation and beamforming. Finally, the position and velocity CRLBs and the simplification-induced error are examined in the simulation. The results demonstrate that the simplified CRLB can be applied in general cases. Based on the simulation results, the impact of resource and geometric parameters on position and velocity error bounds, and the validity of the simplified CRLBs is explained through the corresponding CRLB expressions.

</details>


### [274] [RSMA-Assisted OFDM-OTFS Hybrid Framework for Mixed-Mobility Multiuser Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08532)
*Wafa Hedhly,Leila Musavian,Nikolaos Thomos*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种RSMA辅助的下行通信框架，支持OFDM与OTFS用户在6G车载网络中共存，重点解决多普勒效应导致的ICI问题。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文完全聚焦于6G车载通信中的波形设计、多址接入和信道估计问题，与遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱、空间在轨智能等方向毫无关联。不涉及任何目标检测任务，也不涉及模型轻量化、剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、边缘部署等任何内容。

Model architecture: 提出RSMA辅助系统框架：公共流（common stream）承载OFDM用户的公共消息，占用全带宽以帮助OFDM用户应对高多普勒ICI；OFDM用户的私有流与OTFS用户的流在不相交的频带上传输。

Lightweight method: 不涉及任何模型轻量化方法。无剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等内容。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明任何与星上/边缘部署相关的信息。无参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等指标。

Datasets and metrics: 无遥感或目标检测数据集。评价指标为outage probability（中断概率）和rate performance（速率性能），通过仿真获得结果。

Experiments: 仿真实验分析了功率分配因子和信道估计误差对系统性能的影响，并证明了所提框架在中断概率和速率性能上优于正交复用方案。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，不存在该方向上的局限性讨论。

Abstract: In future 6G vehicular networks, users employing orthogonal frequency division multiplexing (OFDM) and orthogonal time frequency space (OTFS) waveforms may coexist under diverse mobility conditions, where both can experience high-mobility and low-mobility profiles. Since OFDM users can suffer severe inter-carrier interference (ICI) and OTFS users occupy larger spectrum resources, rate-splitting multiple access (RSMA) is a flexible framework that can efficiently handle these heterogeneous aspects. In this work, we propose a novel RSMA-assisted system to provide downlink communication to multiple OFDM and OTFS users. A common stream comprising the common messages of OFDM users spans the whole bandwidth to help OFDM users manage the ICI induced by potential high Doppler effects. OTFS users do not participate in the common stream. The private streams of OFDM users and the streams of OTFS users are transmitted over disjoint frequency bands. During the SIC process implemented at all receivers, channel estimation errors are taken into account. The simulation results highlight the impact of the power allocation factors and channel estimation errors on the system performance, and demonstrate the superiority of the proposed framework over orthogonal multiplexing in terms of outage probability and rate performance.

</details>


### [275] [Adaptive Wavelet Division Multiplexing for Heterogeneous Mobility Users](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08611)
*Seyyed Erfan Fatemi,Wafa Hedhly,Leila Musavian,Nikolaos Thomos*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种基于自适应小波变换的无线通信多用户复用方案，用于异构移动性场景下的频率选择性瑞利衰落信道。

Abstract: This paper proposes an adaptive wavelet division multiplexing scheme for wireless systems serving users with heterogeneous mobility profiles over frequency-selective Rayleigh fading channels. By exploiting the multiresolution structure of the discrete wavelet transform (DWT), users are adaptively assigned to different decomposition levels according to their channel dynamics and Doppler conditions. A single-tap minimum mean square error (MMSE) equalizer is applied in the frequency domain, and the system performance is evaluated under realistic time-varying multipath fading environments. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed adaptive allocation achieves balanced bit error rate (BER) across all user mobility classes while delivering substantial peak-to-average power ratio (PAPR) reductions relative to both conventional orthogonal frequency division multiplexing (OFDM) and orthogonal time-frequency space (OTFS) modulation. The proposed framework is further validated in a four-user heterogeneous-mobility scenario, confirming its scalability and effectiveness to mixed-mobility multi-user scenarios.

</details>


### [276] [Parasitic MIMO Beamforming for Multi-Active Multi-Parasitic Antenna Arrays with Binary Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08624)
*Taejun Lee,Byunghyun Lee,Thomas E. Roth,David J. Love*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种带二进制控制器的多主动多寄生天线(MAMP)架构，通过离散电抗状态和两种波束赋形码本设计，在减少RF链路数量的同时实现与全主动天线阵列相当的波束赋形性能。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于6G通信中的MIMO天线架构和波束赋形码本设计，属于无线通信物理层领域，完全不涉及遥感、卫星在轨目标检测或模型轻量化。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明。论文任务为MIMO波束赋形码本设计，应用场景为6G通信，不涉及遥感、目标检测或任何视觉任务。

Model architecture: 多主动多寄生天线(MAMP)架构，每个寄生元件工作在两种离散电抗状态之一，由二进制控制器控制。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明。论文通过HFSS仿真验证天线辐射模式，并在多种信道场景下进行仿真评估，不涉及遥感或目标检测数据集。

Abstract: In 6G, MIMO dimensions continue to scale, yet the increased cost, power consumption, and hardware complexity associated with growing RF chains limit practical deployment. Parasitic antennas offer a promising alternative that can add spatial degrees of freedom and array gain without a proportional increase in RF chains. From a communication perspective, prior work on parasitic antennas has primarily focused on adjusting continuous reactance values using varactors, but such varactor-based tuning has increased cost and complexity in the analog control and practical RF circuit design. This paper proposes a multi-active multi-parasitic antenna (MAMP) architecture with binary controllers, where each parasitic element operates in one of two discrete reactance states. To validate the practicality of the system, we experimentally identify array geometries that best match the actual radiation patterns with those of the mathematical model through HFSS simulations. We express the induced current vector as a quadratic function of the binary state vector, and propose a pair of discrete reactance values that minimize the relative error of the proposed model while being implementable with off-the-shelf RF components. With these results, we develop two transmit beamforming codebook designs based on the generalized Lloyd algorithm. The first design exhaustively searches for all possible binary combinations to find the optimal solution, representing the theoretical upper limits of our framework. The second design leverages eigenvalue perturbation to significantly reduce computational complexity, making it suitable for online adaptation. Extensive simulations under various channel scenarios demonstrate that the proposed codebook designs enable MAMP with only few active antennas to achieve beamforming performance comparable to fully active antenna arrays with significantly more active antennas.

</details>
